    33	
    34	void fill(double* values, const index_t count, const double maxVal) {
    35	    for (index_t i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    36	        values[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));
    37	    }
    38	}
    39	
    40	// Preserve the original deterministic matrix construction, while doing all
    41	// size arithmetic wide enough to avoid overflow for dimensions above 65535.
    42	void initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters,
    43	                      const index_t nonzeros, const index_t dim) {
    44	    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;
    45	    const std::uint64_t matrixEntries =
    46	        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(dim) * static_cast<std::uint64_t>(dim);
    47	    const double probability = static_cast<double>(nonzeros) /
    48	                               static_cast<double>(matrixEntries);
    49	
    50	    srand(8675309);
    51	    bool fillRemaining = false;
    52	    for (index_t row = 0; row < dim; ++row) {
    53	        rowDelimiters[row] = nnzAssigned;
    54	        for (index_t col = 0; col < dim; ++col) {
    55	            const std::uint64_t position =
    56	                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(row) * dim + col;
    57	            const std::uint64_t entriesLeft = matrixEntries - position;
    58	            const std::uint64_t needed = nonzeros - nnzAssigned;
    59	            if (entriesLeft <= needed) {
    60	                fillRemaining = true;
    61	            }
    62	
    63	            const double randomValue = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;
    64	            if ((nnzAssigned < nonzeros && randomValue <= probability) ||
    65	                fillRemaining) {
    66	                cols[nnzAssigned++] = col;
    67	            }
    68	        }
    69	    }
    70	    rowDelimiters[dim] = nonzeros;
    71	}
    72	
    73	// Each rank invokes this kernel only for the CSR rows assigned to that rank.
    74	// Contiguous rows retain the exact accumulation order of the serial program.
    75	void spmvCpu(const double* __restrict__ values,
    76	             const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
    77	             const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
    78	             const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rowCount,
    79	             double* __restrict__ out) {
    80	    for (index_t row = 0; row < rowCount; ++row) {
    81	        double sum = 0.0;
    82	        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
    83	        for (index_t entry = rowDelimiters[row]; entry < end; ++entry) {
    84	            sum += values[entry] * vec[cols[entry]];
    85	        }
    86	        out[row] = sum;
    87	    }
    88	}
    89	
    90	bool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result,
    91	                   const index_t size) {
    92	    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
    93	        const double ref = reference[i];
    94	        const double res = result[i];
    95	        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {
    96	            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
    97	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\n",
    98	                            i, ref, res);
    99	                return false;
   100	            }
   101	        } else {
   102	            const double relativeError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);
   103	            if (relativeError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {
   104	                std::printf("Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e "
   105	                            "(rel error: %.10e)\n",
   106	                            i, ref, res, relativeError);
   107	                return false;
   108	            }
   109	        }
   110	    }
   111	    return true;
   112	}
   113	
   114	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   115	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   116	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   117	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\n");
   118	    std::printf("  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\n");
   119	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
   120	    std::printf("  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\n");
   121	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   122	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   123	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   124	}
   125	
   126	bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
   127	    if (text[0] == '-') {
   128	        return false;
   129	    }
   130	    errno = 0;
   131	    char* end = nullptr;
   132	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   133	    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' ||
   134	        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   135	        return false;
   136	    }
   137	    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
   138	    return true;
   139	}
   140	
   141	Config parseArguments(const int argc, char** argv) {
   142	    Config config;
   143	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   144	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   145	            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], &config.numRows)) {
   146	                config.parseResult = 2;
   147	                break;
   148	            }
   149	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   150	            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], &config.sparsity)) {
   151	                config.parseResult = 2;
   152	                break;
   153	            }
   154	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   155	            if (!parseIndex(argv[++i], &config.iterations)) {
   156	                config.parseResult = 2;
   157	                break;
   158	            }
   159	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-m") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   160	            errno = 0;
   161	            char* end = nullptr;
   162	            config.maxVal = std::strtod(argv[++i], &end);
   163	            if (errno != 0 || end == argv[i] || *end != '\0' ||
   164	                !std::isfinite(config.maxVal)) {
   165	                config.parseResult = 2;
   166	                break;
   167	            }
   168	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   169	            config.validate = 1;
   170	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   171	            config.printResults = 1;
   172	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   173	            config.parseResult = 1;
   174	            break;
   175	        } else {
   176	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Unknown or incomplete option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   177	            config.parseResult = 2;
   178	            break;
   179	        }
   180	    }
   181	
   182	    if (config.parseResult == 0 &&
   183	        (config.numRows == 0 || config.sparsity == 0 || config.iterations == 0)) {
   184	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size, sparsity, and iterations must be positive.\n");
   185	        config.parseResult = 2;
   186	    }
   187	    if (config.parseResult == 0) {
   188	        const std::uint64_t matrixEntries =
   189	            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.numRows) * config.numRows;
   190	        if (matrixEntries / config.sparsity >
   191	            std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
   192	            std::fprintf(stderr,
   193	                         "The requested matrix has too many nonzeros for the CSR index type.\n");
   194	            config.parseResult = 2;
   195	        }
   196	    }
   197	    return config;
   198	}
   199	
   200	// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
   201	// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
   202	// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
   203	std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
   204	                                   const index_t numRows,
   205	                                   const index_t nonzeros,
   206	                                   const int processCount) {
   207	    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
   208	    starts.front() = 0;
   209	    starts.back() = numRows;
   210	
   211	    if (nonzeros == 0) {
   212	        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
   213	            starts[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
   214	                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) * rank / processCount);
   215	        }
   216	        return starts;
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
   220	        const std::uint64_t target =
   221	            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank / processCount;
   222	        const index_t previous = starts[rank - 1];
   223	        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
   224	        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
   225	        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
   226	        if (upper > numRows) {
   227	            upper = numRows;
   228	        }
   229	        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
   230	        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
   231	            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
   232	                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
   233	        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
   234	            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
   235	                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
   236	        starts[rank] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
   237	    }
   238	    return starts;
   239	}
   240	
   241	template <typename T>
   242	void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
   243	                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
   244	    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
   245	    while (offset < count) {
   246	        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
   247	            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
   248	        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
   249	        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   250	    }
   251	}
   252	
   253	template <typename T>
   254	void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
   255	                       std::vector<T>& local,
   256	                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
   257	                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
   258	                       const int rank, const int processCount,
   259	                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
   260	    const std::uint64_t localCount =
   261	        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rank + 1]) - starts[rank];
   262	    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
   263	
   264	    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   265	        std::vector<int> counts;
   266	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   267	        if (rank == 0) {
   268	            counts.resize(processCount);
   269	            displacements.resize(processCount);
   270	            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
   271	                counts[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer + 1] - starts[peer]);
   272	                displacements[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer]);
   273	            }
   274	        }
   275	        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
   276	                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   277	                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
   278	                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
   279	                     communicator);
   280	        return;
   281	    }
   282	
   283	    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
   284	    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
   285	    if (rank == 0) {
   286	        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
   287	                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
   288	        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
   289	            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peer];
   290	            std::uint64_t remaining =
   291	                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peer + 1]) - starts[peer];
   292	            while (remaining != 0) {
   293	                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
   294	                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
   295	                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
   296	                         communicator);
   297	                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   298	                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   299	            }
   300	        }
   301	    } else {
   302	        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
   303	        while (offset < localCount) {
   304	            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
   305	                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
   306	            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
   307	                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   308	            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   309	        }
   310	    }
   311	}
   312	
   313	template <typename T>
   314	void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
   315	                      std::vector<T>& global,
   316	                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
   317	                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
   318	                      const int rank, const int processCount,
   319	                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
   320	    const std::uint64_t localCount =
   321	        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rank + 1]) - starts[rank];
   322	    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
   323	
   324	    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
   325	        std::vector<int> counts;
   326	        std::vector<int> displacements;
   327	        if (rank == 0) {
   328	            counts.resize(processCount);
   329	            displacements.resize(processCount);
   330	            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
   331	                counts[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer + 1] - starts[peer]);
   332	                displacements[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer]);
   333	            }
   334	        }
   335	        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
   336	                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
   337	                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   338	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
   339	                    communicator);
   340	        return;
   341	    }
   342	
   343	    if (rank == 0) {
   344	        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
   345	                    global.data() + starts[0]);
   346	        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
   347	            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peer];
   348	            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peer + 1];
   349	            while (offset < end) {
   350	                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
   351	                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
   352	                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
   353	                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   354	                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   355	            }
   356	        }
   357	    } else {
   358	        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
   359	        while (offset < localCount) {
   360	            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
   361	                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
   362	            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
   363	                     communicator);
   364	            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
   365	        }
   366	    }
   367	}
   368	
   369	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   370	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   371	
   372	    MPI_Comm communicator = MPI_COMM_WORLD;
   373	    int rank = 0;
   374	    int processCount = 1;
   375	    MPI_Comm_rank(communicator, &rank);
   376	    MPI_Comm_size(communicator, &processCount);
   377	
   378	    Config config;
   379	    if (rank == 0) {
   380	        config = parseArguments(argc, argv);
   381	    }
   382	    MPI_Bcast(&config, static_cast<int>(sizeof(config)), MPI_BYTE, 0,
   383	              communicator);
   384	
   385	    if (config.parseResult != 0) {
   386	        if (rank == 0) {
   387	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   388	        }
   389	        MPI_Finalize();
   390	        return config.parseResult == 1 ? 0 : 1;
   391	    }
   392	
   393	    const index_t numRows = config.numRows;
   394	    const std::uint64_t matrixEntries =
   395	        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) * numRows;
   396	    const index_t nonzeros =
   397	        static_cast<index_t>(matrixEntries / config.sparsity);
   398	
   399	    if (rank == 0) {
   400	        std::printf("Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\n");
   401	        std::printf("Matrix size: %u x %u\n", numRows, numRows);
   402	        std::printf("Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\n",
   403	                    config.sparsity);
   404	        std::printf("Non-zero elements: %u (%.2f%% sparse)\n", nonzeros,
   405	                    100.0 * (1.0 - static_cast<double>(nonzeros) /
   406	                                       static_cast<double>(matrixEntries)));
   407	        std::printf("Iterations: %u\n", config.iterations);
   408	        std::printf("Max value: %.2f\n", config.maxVal);
   409	        std::printf("MPI processes: %d\n", processCount);
   410	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n",
   411	                    config.validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   412	        std::printf("Initializing data structures...\n");
   413	    }
   414	
   415	    std::vector<double> vec(numRows);
   416	    std::vector<double> globalValues;
   417	    std::vector<index_t> globalColumns;
   418	    std::vector<index_t> globalRowDelimiters;
   419	    std::vector<double> reference;
   420	
   421	    if (rank == 0) {
   422	        globalValues.resize(nonzeros);
   423	        globalColumns.resize(nonzeros);
   424	        globalRowDelimiters.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(numRows) + 1);
   425	
   426	        // C specifies the initial rand() state as if seeded with one. Make it
   427	        // explicit so MPI/runtime initialization cannot affect reproducibility.
   428	        srand(1);
   429	        fill(vec.data(), numRows, config.maxVal);
   430	        fill(globalValues.data(), nonzeros, config.maxVal);
   431	        initRandomMatrix(globalColumns.data(), globalRowDelimiters.data(),
   432	                         nonzeros, numRows);
   433	
   434	        if (config.validate) {
   435	            std::printf("Computing reference solution...\n");
   436	            reference.resize(numRows);
   437	            spmvCpu(globalValues.data(), globalColumns.data(),
   438	                    globalRowDelimiters.data(), vec.data(), numRows,
   439	                    reference.data());
   440	        }
   441	    }
   442	
   443	    broadcastLarge(vec.data(), numRows, MPI_DOUBLE, communicator);
   444	
   445	    std::vector<index_t> rowStarts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
   446	    std::vector<index_t> nnzStarts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
   447	    if (rank == 0) {
   448	        rowStarts = partitionRows(globalRowDelimiters, numRows, nonzeros,
   449	                                  processCount);
   450	        for (int peer = 0; peer <= processCount; ++peer) {
   451	            nnzStarts[peer] = globalRowDelimiters[rowStarts[peer]];
   452	        }
   453	    }
   454	    MPI_Bcast(rowStarts.data(), processCount + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
   455	              communicator);
   456	    MPI_Bcast(nnzStarts.data(), processCount + 1, MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
   457	              communicator);
   458	
   459	    const index_t localRows = rowStarts[rank + 1] - rowStarts[rank];
   460	    const index_t localNonzeros = nnzStarts[rank + 1] - nnzStarts[rank];
   461	    std::vector<double> localValues(localNonzeros);
   462	    std::vector<index_t> localColumns(localNonzeros);
   463	    std::vector<index_t> localRowDelimiters(
   464	        static_cast<std::size_t>(localRows) + 1);
   465	    std::vector<double> localOutput(localRows);
   466	
   467	    scatterContiguous(globalValues, localValues, nnzStarts, MPI_DOUBLE,
   468	                      VALUES_TAG, rank, processCount, communicator);
   469	    scatterContiguous(globalColumns, localColumns, nnzStarts, MPI_UINT32_T,
   470	                      COLUMNS_TAG, rank, processCount, communicator);
   471	    scatterContiguous(globalRowDelimiters, localRowDelimiters, rowStarts,
   472	                      MPI_UINT32_T, ROWS_TAG, rank, processCount, communicator);
   473	
   474	    const index_t globalNnzOffset = nnzStarts[rank];
   475	    for (index_t row = 0; row < localRows; ++row) {
   476	        localRowDelimiters[row] -= globalNnzOffset;
   477	    }
   478	    localRowDelimiters[localRows] = localNonzeros;
   479	
   480	    // The global CSR is no longer needed during the benchmark. Releasing it
   481	    // avoids retaining an unnecessary full-matrix copy on rank zero.
   482	    if (rank == 0) {
   483	        std::vector<double>().swap(globalValues);
   484	        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalColumns);
   485	        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalRowDelimiters);
   486	        std::printf("Computing SpMV...\n");
   487	    }
   488	
   489	    MPI_Barrier(communicator);
   490	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   491	    for (index_t iteration = 0; iteration < config.iterations; ++iteration) {
   492	        spmvCpu(localValues.data(), localColumns.data(),
   493	                localRowDelimiters.data(), vec.data(), localRows,
   494	                localOutput.data());
   495	    }
   496	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   497	
   498	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   499	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   500	               communicator);
   501	
   502	    if (rank == 0) {
   503	        const double milliseconds = elapsed * 1000.0;
   504	        const double averageMilliseconds =
   505	            milliseconds / static_cast<double>(config.iterations);
   506	        const double gflops = elapsed > 0.0
   507	                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(nonzeros) *
   508	                                     config.iterations) /
   509	                                        elapsed / 1e9
   510	                                  : 0.0;
   511	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
   512	        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n",
   513	                    averageMilliseconds);
   514	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   515	    }
   516	
   517	    std::vector<double> globalOutput;
   518	    if (config.printResults || config.validate) {
   519	        if (rank == 0) {
   520	            globalOutput.resize(numRows);
   521	        }
   522	        gatherContiguous(localOutput, globalOutput, rowStarts, MPI_DOUBLE,
   523	                         OUTPUT_TAG, rank, processCount, communicator);
   524	    }
   525	
   526	    int exitCode = 0;
   527	    if (rank == 0) {
   528	        if (config.printResults) {
   529	            print_results(globalOutput, "OutputVector");
   530	        }
   531	        if (config.validate) {
   532	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   533	            if (verifyResults(reference.data(), globalOutput.data(), numRows)) {
   534	                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   535	            } else {
   536	                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   537	                exitCode = 1;
   538	            }
   539	        }
   540	    }
   541	    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
   542	    MPI_Finalize();
   543	    return exitCode;
   544	}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {

+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            starts[rank] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) * rank / processCount);
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) * rank / processCount;
+        const index_t previous = starts[rank - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rank] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rank + 1]) - starts[rank];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(processCount);
+            displacements.resize(processCount);
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                counts[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer + 1] - starts[peer]);
+                displacements[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peer];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peer + 1]) - starts[peer];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rank + 1]) - starts[rank];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(processCount);
+            displacements.resize(processCount);
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                counts[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer + 1] - starts[peer]);
+                displacements[peer] = static_cast<int>(starts[peer]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peer];
+            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peer + 1];
+            while (offset < end) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}

+void spmvCpu(const double* __restrict__ values,

+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+            starts[rankIndex] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) *
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount));
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) *
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount);
+        const index_t previous = starts[rankIndex - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rankIndex] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peerIndex + 1]) -
+                starts[peerIndex];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peerIndex + 1];
+            while (offset < end) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+// Each rank invokes this kernel only for the CSR rows assigned to that rank.
+// Contiguous rows retain the exact accumulation order of the serial program.
+void spmvCpu(const double* __restrict__ values,
+             const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+             const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
+             const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t rowCount,
+             double* __restrict__ out) {
+    for (index_t row = 0; row < rowCount; ++row) {
+        double sum = 0.0;
+        const index_t end = rowDelimiters[row + 1];
+        for (index_t entry = rowDelimiters[row]; entry < end; ++entry) {
+            sum += values[entry] * vec[cols[entry]];
         }
-        out[i] = t;
+        out[row] = sum;
     }
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;
+    }
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);

+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    *value = static_cast<index_t>(parsed);
+    return true;

+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+            starts[rankIndex] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) *
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount));
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) *
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount);
+        const index_t previous = starts[rankIndex - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rankIndex] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peerIndex + 1]) -
+                starts[peerIndex];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peerIndex + 1];
+            while (offset < end) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}

+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               communicator);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const double milliseconds = elapsed * 1000.0;
+        const double averageMilliseconds =
+            milliseconds / static_cast<double>(config.iterations);
+        const double gflops = elapsed > 0.0
+                                  ? (2.0 * static_cast<double>(nonzeros) *
+                                     config.iterations) /
+                                        elapsed / 1e9
+                                  : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Average time per iteration: %.3f ms\n",
+                    averageMilliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> globalOutput;
+    if (config.printResults || config.validate) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            globalOutput.resize(numRows);
         }
+        gatherContiguous(localOutput, globalOutput, rowStarts, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         OUTPUT_TAG, rank, processCount, communicator);
     }
 
-    return 0;
+    int exitCode = 0;
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        if (config.printResults) {
+            print_results(globalOutput, "OutputVector");
+        }
+        if (config.validate) {
+            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+            if (verifyResults(reference.data(), globalOutput.data(), numRows)) {
+                std::printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+            } else {
+                std::printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
+                exitCode = 1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, communicator);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return exitCode;
 }

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {

+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+            starts[rankIndex] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) *
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount));
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) *
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount);
+        const index_t previous = starts[rankIndex - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rankIndex] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peerIndex + 1]) -
+                starts[peerIndex];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);

+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));

+void spmvCpu(const double* __restrict__ values,

+             const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {

+        (config.numRows == 0 || config.sparsity == 0 || config.iterations == 0)) {
+        std::fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size, sparsity, and iterations must be positive.\n");
+        config.parseResult = 2;

+        const std::uint64_t matrixEntries =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(config.numRows) * config.numRows;
+        if (matrixEntries / config.sparsity >
+            std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr,
+                         "The requested matrix has too many nonzeros for the CSR index type.\n");
+            config.parseResult = 2;
+        }
+    }
+    return config;
+}
+
+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+            starts[rankIndex] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) *
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount));
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) *
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount);
+        const index_t previous = starts[rankIndex - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rankIndex] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peerIndex + 1]) -
+                starts[peerIndex];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peerIndex + 1];
+            while (offset < end) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}

+bool parseIndex(const char* text, index_t* value) {
+    if (text[0] == '-') {
+        return false;

+        parsed > std::numeric_limits<index_t>::max()) {

+// Choose contiguous row boundaries closest to equal cumulative-nonzero
+// targets. This keeps the numerical ordering stable while balancing work even
+// when the random matrix has uneven row densities.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t numRows,
+                                   const index_t nonzeros,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> starts(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    starts.front() = 0;
+    starts.back() = numRows;
+
+    if (nonzeros == 0) {
+        for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+            const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+            starts[rankIndex] = static_cast<index_t>(
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(numRows) *
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount));
+        }
+        return starts;
+    }
+
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+        const std::uint64_t target =
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(nonzeros) *
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(rank) /
+            static_cast<std::uint64_t>(processCount);
+        const index_t previous = starts[rankIndex - 1];
+        const auto first = rowDelimiters.begin() + previous;
+        const auto found = std::lower_bound(first, rowDelimiters.end(), target);
+        index_t upper = static_cast<index_t>(found - rowDelimiters.begin());
+        if (upper > numRows) {
+            upper = numRows;
+        }
+        const index_t lower = upper > previous ? upper - 1 : upper;
+        const std::uint64_t lowerDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[lower] ? target - rowDelimiters[lower]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[lower] - target;
+        const std::uint64_t upperDistance =
+            target >= rowDelimiters[upper] ? target - rowDelimiters[upper]
+                                           : rowDelimiters[upper] - target;
+        starts[rankIndex] = lowerDistance <= upperDistance ? lower : upper;
+    }
+    return starts;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void broadcastLarge(T* data, const std::uint64_t count,
+                    const MPI_Datatype datatype, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+    while (offset < count) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+            std::min<std::uint64_t>(count - offset, INT_MAX));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, communicator);
+        offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+    }
+}

+template <typename T>
+void scatterContiguous(const std::vector<T>& global,
+                       std::vector<T>& local,
+                       const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                       const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                       const int rank, const int processCount,
+                       MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Scatterv(rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype,
+                     local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype, 0,
+                     communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    // MPI-3 collectives use int counts/displacements. Retain support for the
+    // full uint32_t CSR range by chunking only when those limits are exceeded.
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(global.data() + starts[0],
+                    static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount), local.data());
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            std::uint64_t remaining =
+                static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[peerIndex + 1]) -
+                starts[peerIndex];
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(remaining, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Send(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+                remaining -= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Recv(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void gatherContiguous(const std::vector<T>& local,
+                      std::vector<T>& global,
+                      const std::vector<index_t>& starts,
+                      const MPI_Datatype datatype, const int tag,
+                      const int rank, const int processCount,
+                      MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    const auto rankIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(rank);
+    const std::uint64_t localCount =
+        static_cast<std::uint64_t>(starts[rankIndex + 1]) - starts[rankIndex];
+    const std::uint64_t totalCount = starts.back();
+
+    if (totalCount <= static_cast<std::uint64_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        std::vector<int> counts;
+        std::vector<int> displacements;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            displacements.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(processCount));
+            for (int peer = 0; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+                const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+                counts[peerIndex] = static_cast<int>(
+                    starts[peerIndex + 1] - starts[peerIndex]);
+                displacements[peerIndex] =
+                    static_cast<int>(starts[peerIndex]);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(local.data(), static_cast<int>(localCount), datatype,
+                    rank == 0 ? global.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, datatype, 0,
+                    communicator);
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::copy_n(local.data(), static_cast<std::size_t>(localCount),
+                    global.data() + starts[0]);
+        for (int peer = 1; peer < processCount; ++peer) {
+            const auto peerIndex = static_cast<std::size_t>(peer);
+            std::uint64_t offset = starts[peerIndex];
+            const std::uint64_t end = starts[peerIndex + 1];
+            while (offset < end) {
+                const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                    std::min<std::uint64_t>(end - offset, INT_MAX));
+                MPI_Recv(global.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, peer, tag,
+                         communicator, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        std::uint64_t offset = 0;
+        while (offset < localCount) {
+            const int chunk = static_cast<int>(
+                std::min<std::uint64_t>(localCount - offset, INT_MAX));
+            MPI_Send(local.data() + offset, chunk, datatype, 0, tag,
+                     communicator);
+            offset += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(chunk);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Each rank invokes this kernel only for the CSR rows assigned to that rank.
+// Contiguous rows retain the exact accumulation order of the serial program.
+void spmvCpu(const double* __restrict__ values,
+             const index_t* __restrict__ cols,
+             const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,
